  function SaveSettings()

      return false
    end

      writefile("meolazy/Settings.json", (httpService2:JSONEncode(_G.SaveData)))
    end))
  end

  function LoadSettings()
    if not (isfile and isfile("meolazy/Settings.json")) then
      return false
    else
      local v2, v3 = pcall(function()
        return httpService2:JSONDecode((readfile("meolazy/Settings.json")))
      end)

      if v2 and v3 then
        _G.SaveData = v3
        return true
      end

      return false
    end
  end

  function GetSetting(p1, p2)
    return _G.SaveData[p1] ~= nil and _G.SaveData[p1] or p2
  end

  function EquipWeapon(p3)
    if not p3 then
      return
    end

    if plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p3) then
      plr.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p3))
    end
  end

  function weaponSc(p4)
    for key2, value in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
      if value:IsA("Tool") then
        if value.ToolTip == p4 then
          EquipWeapon(value.Name)
        end
      end
    end
  end

  _G.MobHeight = _G.MobHeight or 20

  function v8.Kill(p12, p13)
    if not (p12 and p13) then
      return
    else
      local humanoidRootPart = p12:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if not humanoidRootPart then
        return
      end

      if not p12:GetAttribute("Locked") then
        p12:SetAttribute("Locked", humanoidRootPart.CFrame)
      end

      PosMon = p12:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
      _B = true
      BringEnemy()
      EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

      if not players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
        return
      end

      _tp(humanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, _G.MobHeight, 0))
      return
    end
  end

  function v8.Kill2(p14, p15)
    if p14 and p15 then
      if not p14:GetAttribute("Locked") then
        p14:SetAttribute("Locked", p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
      end

      PosMon = p14:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
      BringEnemy()
      EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)

      local equippedTool = players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
      if equippedTool and equippedTool.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0)
          * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0))
      else
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 8)
          * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
      end

      if RandomCFrame then
        wait(0.1)
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
        wait(0.1)
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
        wait(0.1)
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
        wait(0.1)
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
        wait(0.1)
        _tp(p14.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
      end
    end
  end

  function v8.KillSea(p16, p17)

function v8.Sword(p18, p19)
  if p18 and p19 then
    if not p18:GetAttribute("Locked") then
      p18:SetAttribute("Locked", p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)

    weaponSc("Sword")

    if RandomCFrame then
      wait(0.1)
      _tp(p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
      wait(0.1)
      _tp(p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(25, 30, 0))
      wait(0.1)
      _tp(p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
      wait(0.1)
      _tp(p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 25))
      wait(0.1)
      _tp(p18.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(-25, 30, 0))
    end
  end
end

_G.FruitSkills = {
  Z = false,
  X = false,
  C = false,
  V = false,
  F = false,
}

function UseFruitSkills()
  weaponSc("Blox Fruit")

  if _G.FruitSkills.Z then
    Useskills("Blox Fruit", "Z")
  end

  if _G.FruitSkills.X then
    Useskills("Blox Fruit", "X")
  end

  if _G.FruitSkills.C then
    Useskills("Blox Fruit", "C")
  end

  if _G.FruitSkills.V then
    Useskills("Blox Fruit", "V")
  end

  if _G.FruitSkills.F then
    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "F", false, game)
    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "F", false, game)
  end
end

function v8.Mas(p20, p21)
  if p20 and p21 then
    if not p20:GetAttribute("Locked") then
      p20:SetAttribute("Locked", p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
    end

    PosMon = p20:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
    BringEnemy()

    if p20.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
      _tp(p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
      UseFruitSkills()
    else
      weaponSc("Melee")
      _tp(p20.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
    end
  end
end

function v8.Masgun(p22, p23)
  if p22 and p23 then
    if not p22:GetAttribute("Locked") then
      p22:SetAttribute("Locked", p22.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
    end

    PosMon = p22:GetAttribute("Locked").Position
    BringEnemy()

    if p22.Humanoid.Health <= HealthM then
      _tp(p22.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 35, 8))
      Useskills("Gun", "Z")
      Useskills("Gun", "X")
    else
      weaponSc("Melee")
      _tp(p22.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
    end
  end
end

function statsSetings(p24, p25)
  if p24 == "Melee" then
    if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Melee", p25)
    end
  elseif p24 == "Defense" then
    if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Defense", p25)
    end
  elseif p24 == "Sword" then
    if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Sword", p25)
    end
  elseif p24 == "Gun" then
    if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Gun", p25)
    end
  elseif p24 == "Devil" then
    if plr.Data.Points.Value ~= 0 then
      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AddPoint", "Demon Fruit", p25)
    end
  end
end

  local function f5(p27)
    local lower = p27.Name:lower()

    if lower:find("raid") or lower:find("microchip") or lower:find("island") then
      return true
    end

    if p27:GetAttribute("IsRaid") or p27:GetAttribute("RaidMob") or p27:GetAttribute("IsBoss") then
      return true
    else
      local humanoid2 = p27:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")

      if humanoid2 and humanoid2.WalkSpeed == 0 then
        return true
      end

      if p27.Parent and tostring(p27.Parent):lower():find("_worldorigin") then
        return true
      end

      return false
    end
  end

  local v151 = false

  function BringEnemy()
    local acceptQuest = _G.AcceptQuest and not f4()
    local localPlayer2

    if acceptQuest then
      v151 = false
      return
    elseif v151 then
      return
    else
      v151 = true
      localPlayer2 = players.LocalPlayer
      local character3 = localPlayer2.Character

      local humanoidRootPart3 = character3
      humanoidRootPart3 = character3 and character3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if not humanoidRootPart3 then
        v151 = false
        return
      else
        pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(localPlayer2, "SimulationRadius", math.huge) end)
        local bringRange = _G.BringRange or 235
        local enemies = workspace.Enemies
        local v152 = {}

        for index, value3 in ipairs((enemies:GetChildren())) do
          local humanoid3 = value3:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
          local humanoidRootPart4 = value3:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

          if humanoid3 and humanoidRootPart4 and humanoid3.Health > 0 and not f5(value3) then
            local v153 = f3(humanoidRootPart4)

            if (humanoidRootPart4.Position - humanoidRootPart3.Position).Magnitude <= bringRange
              and not v153 then
              table.insert(v152, humanoidRootPart4)
            end
          end
        end

        if #v152 == 0 then
          v151 = false
          return
        else
          local v154 = v152[1]

          if not v154 or not v154.Parent then
            v151 = false
            return
          else
            local position = v154.Position

            humanoidRootPart3.CFrame = CFrame.new(position
              + Vector3.new(0, _G.MobHeight or 30, 0))

            local v155 = tick() * 4
            local v156 = #v152 - 1

            for i = 1, #v152 do
              local v157 = v152[i]

              if v157 ~= v154 then
                local v158 = (i - 1) / math.max(v156, 1) * (math.pi * 2) + v155

                v157.CFrame = CFrame.new(position
                  + Vector3.new(math.cos(v158) * 10, 0, math.sin(v158) * 10))

                v157.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                v157.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
              else
                v157.CFrame = CFrame.new(position)
                v157.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                v157.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
              end
            end

            v151 = false
            return
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(1) do
      if _G.AcceptQuest and not f4() then
        _B = false
        v151 = false
        PosMon = nil
        task.wait(0.5)
      elseif f4() then
        _B = true
        BringEnemy()
      else
        _B = false
        task.wait(0.5)
      end
    end
  end)

  function Useskills(p28, p29)
    if p28 == "Melee" then
      weaponSc("Melee")

      if p29 == "Z" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "X" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "C" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
      end
    elseif p28 == "Sword" then
      weaponSc("Sword")

      if p29 == "Z" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "X" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
      end
    elseif p28 == "Blox Fruit" then
      weaponSc("Blox Fruit")

      if p29 == "Z" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "X" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "C" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "V" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "V", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "V", false, game)
      end
    elseif p28 == "Gun" then
      weaponSc("Gun")

      if p29 == "Z" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game)
      elseif p29 == "X" then
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game)
      end
    end

    if p28 == "nil" and p29 == "Y" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Y", false, game)
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Y", false, game)
    end
  end

  local v159 = getrawmetatable(game)
  local namecall = v159.__namecall
  setreadonly(v159, false)

  v159.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
    local v160 = getnamecallmethod()
    local v161 = { ... }

    if tostring(v160) == "FireServer" then
      if tostring(v161[1]) == "RemoteEvent" then
        if tostring(v161[2]) ~= "true" and tostring(v161[2]) ~= "false" then
          local farmMasteryG2 = _G.FarmMastery_G and not SoulGuitar
          local v162 = farmMasteryG2

          if not farmMasteryG2 then
            local farmMasteryDev3 = _G.FarmMastery_Dev
            local v163 = farmMasteryDev3

            if not farmMasteryDev3 then
              local farmBlazeEM2 = _G.FarmBlazeEM
              local v164 = farmBlazeEM2

              if not farmBlazeEM2 then
                local prehisSkills2 = _G.Prehis_Skills

                local seaBeast12 = prehisSkills2 or _G.SeaBeast1 or _G.FishBoat or _G.PGB
                  or _G.Leviathan1 or _G.Complete_Trials
                  or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == "AimBots Skill"
                  or _G.AimMethod and ABmethod == "Auto Aimbots"

                v164 = seaBeast12
              end

              v163 = v164
            end

            v162 = v163
          end

          if v162 then
            v161[2] = MousePos
            return namecall(unpack(v161))
          end
        end
      end

      return namecall(...)
    end

    return namecall(...)
  end)
end

function GetConnectionEnemies(p30)
  for key4, value4 in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
    if value4:IsA("Model")
      and (typeof(p30) == "table" and table.find(p30, value4.Name) or value4.Name == p30)
      and value4:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and value4.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
      return value4
    end
  end

  local v165, v166 = workspaceService.Enemies:GetChildren()

  for key5, value5 in next, v165, v166 do
    if value5:IsA("Model")
      and (typeof(p30) == "table" and table.find(p30, value5.Name) or value5.Name == p30)
      and value5:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and value5.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
      return value5
    end
  end
end

function LowCpu()
  local v167 = game
  local lighting3 = v167.Lighting

  local terrain = v167.Workspace.Terrain
  terrain.WaterWaveSize = 0
  terrain.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
  terrain.WaterReflectance = 0
  terrain.WaterTransparency = 0

  local getDescendants = v167.GetDescendants

  lighting3.GlobalShadows = false
  lighting3.FogEnd = 9000000000
  lighting3.Brightness = 1

  settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = "Level01"

  for key6, value6 in pairs(getDescendants(v167)) do
    if value6:IsA("Part") or value6:IsA("Union") or value6:IsA("CornerWedgePart")
      or value6:IsA("TrussPart") then
      value6.Material = "Plastic"
      value6.Reflectance = 0
    elseif value6:IsA("Decal") or value6:IsA("Texture") and true then
      value6.Transparency = 1
    elseif value6:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or value6:IsA("Trail") then
      value6.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0)
    elseif value6:IsA("Explosion") then
      value6.BlastPressure = 1
      value6.BlastRadius = 1
    elseif value6:IsA("Fire") or value6:IsA("SpotLight") or value6:IsA("Smoke")
      or value6:IsA("Sparkles") then
      value6.Enabled = false
    elseif value6:IsA("MeshPart") then
      value6.Material = "Plastic"
      value6.Reflectance = 0
      value6.TextureID = 10385902758728956
    end
  end

  for key7, value7 in pairs(lighting3:GetChildren()) do
    if value7:IsA("BlurEffect") or value7:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
      or value7:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or value7:IsA("BloomEffect")
      or value7:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
      value7.Enabled = false
    end
  end
end

    or GetBP("Kitsune-Kitsune") or GetBP("T-Rex-T-Rex") then
    return true
  end
end

  return false
end

  function UpdStFruit()
    local v168, v169 = plr.Backpack:GetChildren()

    for key16, value16 in next, v168, v169 do
      StoreFruit = value16:FindFirstChild("EatRemote", true)

      if StoreFruit then
        local commF = replicated.Remotes.CommF_
        local parent = StoreFruit.Parent
        local backpack = plr.Backpack

        commF:InvokeServer(
          "StoreFruit", parent:GetAttribute("OriginalName"),
          backpack:FindFirstChild(value16.Name)
        )
      end
    end
  end

      for key17, value17 in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do

          value17.Handle.CFrame = character4.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
        end
      end
    end
  end

  function Getmoon()
    if World1 then
      return lighting2.FantasySky.MoonTextureId
    elseif World2 then
      return lighting2.FantasySky.MoonTextureId
    else
      if World3 then
        return lighting2.Sky.MoonTextureId
      end

      return
    end
  end

  function DropFruits()
    local v170, v171 = plr.Backpack:GetChildren()

    for key18, value18 in next, v170, v171 do
      if string.find(value18.Name, "Fruit") then
        EquipWeapon(value18.Name)
        wait(0.1)

        if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
          plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
        end

        EquipWeapon(value18.Name)
        plr.Character:FindFirstChild(value18.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
      end
    end

    for key19, value19 in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
      if string.find(value19.Name, "Fruit") then
        EquipWeapon(value19.Name)
        wait(0.1)

        if plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible == true then
          plr.PlayerGui.Main.Dialogue.Visible = false
        end

        EquipWeapon(value19.Name)
        plr.Character:FindFirstChild(value19.Name).EatRemote:InvokeServer("Drop")
      end
    end
  end

  function GetBP(p32)

  end

  function GetIn(p33)
    for key20, value20 in pairs(replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")) do

        if value20.Name == p33 or plr.Character:FindFirstChild(p33)
          or plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p33) then
          return true
        end
      end
    end

    return false
  end

  function GetM(p34)

      if type(value21) == "table" then
        if value21.Type == "Material" then
          if value21.Name == p34 then
            return value21.Count
          end
        end
      end
    end

    return 0
  end

  function getInfinity_Ability(p36, p37)
    if not Root then
      return
    end

    local v172

    if p36 == "Soru" and p37 then
      local v173, v174 = getgc()

      for key23, value23 in next, v173, v174 do
        if plr.Character.Soru then
          if typeof(value23) == "function" and getfenv(value23).script == plr.Character.Soru then
            local v175, v176 = getupvalues(value23)

            for key24, value24 in next, v175, v176 do
              if typeof(value24) == "table" then
                repeat
                  wait(Sec)
                  value24.LastUse = 0
                  v172 = not p37 or plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                until v172
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
    elseif p36 == "Energy" and p37 then
      plr.Character.Energy.Changed:connect(function()
        if p37 then
          plr.Character.Energy.Value = Energy
        end
      end)
    elseif p36 == "Observation" and p37 then
      plr.VisionRadius.Value = math.huge
    end
  end

  function Hop()
    pcall(function()
      for j = math.random(1, math.random(40, 75)), 100 do
        for key25, value25 in next, replicated.__ServerBrowser:InvokeServer(j), nil do
          if tonumber(value25.Count) < 12 then
            TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, key25)
          end
        end
      end
    end)
  end

  local ripIndra = Instance.new("Part", workspace)
  ripIndra.Size = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
  ripIndra.Name = "Rip_Indra"
  ripIndra.Anchored = true
  ripIndra.CanCollide = false
  ripIndra.CanTouch = false
  ripIndra.Transparency = 1

  local findFirstChild = workspace:FindFirstChild(ripIndra.Name)

  if findFirstChild and findFirstChild ~= ripIndra then
    findFirstChild:Destroy()
  end

  task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
      if ripIndra and ripIndra.Parent == workspace then
        if shouldTween then
          getgenv().OnFarm = true
        else
          getgenv().OnFarm = false
        end
      else
        getgenv().OnFarm = false
      end
    end
  end)

  task.spawn(function()
    local localPlayer3 = players.LocalPlayer
    local primaryPart

    repeat
      task.wait()
      primaryPart = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character.PrimaryPart
    until primaryPart

    ripIndra.CFrame = localPlayer3.Character.PrimaryPart.CFrame

    while task.wait() do
      pcall(function()
        if getgenv().OnFarm then
          if ripIndra and ripIndra.Parent == workspace then
            local primaryPart2 = localPlayer3.Character and localPlayer3.Character.PrimaryPart

            if primaryPart2 and (primaryPart2.Position - ripIndra.Position).Magnitude <= 200 then
              primaryPart2.CFrame = ripIndra.CFrame
            else
              ripIndra.CFrame = primaryPart2.CFrame
            end
          end

          local character5 = localPlayer3.Character

          if character5 then
            for key26, value26 in pairs(character5:GetChildren()) do
              if value26:IsA("BasePart") then
                value26.CanCollide = false
              end
            end
          end
        else
          local character6 = localPlayer3.Character

          if character6 then
            for key27, value27 in pairs(character6:GetChildren()) do
              if value27:IsA("BasePart") then
                value27.CanCollide = true
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end)
    end
  end)

  getgenv().TweenSpeedFar = 310
  getgenv().TweenSpeedNear = 900

  function _tp(p38)
    local character7 = plr.Character
    local v177 = not character7
    local create

    if v177 or not character7:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
      return
    else
      local humanoidRootPart5 = character7.HumanoidRootPart
      shouldTween = true
      getgenv().OnFarm = false

      if humanoidRootPart5.Anchored then
        humanoidRootPart5.Anchored = false
        task.wait()
      end

      local magnitude = (p38.Position - humanoidRootPart5.Position).Magnitude

      local tweenSpeedNear = magnitude <= 90 and (getgenv().TweenSpeedNear or 900)
        or getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or 310

      create = tweenService:Create(ripIndra, TweenInfo.new(
        magnitude / tweenSpeedNear, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear
      ), { CFrame = p38 })

      if character7.Humanoid.Sit == true then
        ripIndra.CFrame = CFrame.new(ripIndra.Position.X, p38.Y, ripIndra.Position.Z)
      end

      create:Play()

      task.spawn(function()
        while create.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Playing do
          if not shouldTween then
            create:Cancel()
            break
          end

          task.wait(0.1)
        end

        getgenv().OnFarm = true
      end)

      return
    end
  end
end

            plr.Character:FindFirstChild("highlight"):Destroy()
          end
        end
      end)
    end
  end)

  function QuestBeta()

  function QuestCheck()
    local value29 = players.LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value

    if World1 and value29 > 699 then
      value29 = 650
    end

    if World2 and value29 > 1499 then

    end

    if World1 then
      if value29 == 1 or value29 <= 9 then
        if tostring(TeamSelf) == "Marines" then
          Mon = "Trainee"

          if tostring(v347[1]) == "RemoteEvent" or tostring(v347[1]):find("Shoot")
            or tostring(v347[1]):find("Attack") then
            if type(v347[2]) == "Vector3" then
              v347[2] = getgenv().GunAuraSettings.CurrentTarget.Position
            elseif type(v347[3]) == "Vector3" then
              v347[3] = getgenv().GunAuraSettings.CurrentTarget.Position
            end
          end
        end
      end

      return namecall2(unpack(v347))
    end)

    timeZoneAddParagraph = statusServerTab:AddParagraph({ Title = "Time Zone", Desc = "" })

    function UpdateOS()
      date = os.date("*t")
      hour = date.hour % 24
      ampm = hour < 12 and "AM" or "PM"

      timezone = string.format(
        "%02i:%02i:%02i %s", (hour - 1) % 12 + 1, date.min, date.sec, ampm
      )

      datetime = string.format("%02d/%02d/%04d", date.day, date.month, date.year)
      LocalizationService = localizationService
      v317 = players
      localPlayer = v317.LocalPlayer
      name = localPlayer.Name

      result, code = pcall(function()
        return LocalizationService:GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(localPlayer)
      end)

      timeZoneAddParagraph:SetDesc(datetime .. " - " .. timezone .. " [ " .. code .. " ]")
    end

    spawn(function()
      while wait() do
        UpdateOS()
        runService.RenderStepped:Wait()
      end
    end)

    timeAddParagraph = statusServerTab:AddParagraph({ Title = "Time", Desc = "" })

    function UpdateTime()
      GameTime = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime + 0.5)
      Hour = math.floor(GameTime / 3600) % 24
      Minute = math.floor(GameTime / 60) % 60
      Second = GameTime % 60

      timeAddParagraph:SetDesc(Hour .. " Hour (h) " .. Minute .. " Minute (m) " .. Second
        .. " Second (s) ")
    end

    spawn(function()
      while wait() do
        UpdateTime()
        wait()
      end
    end)

    function f8()
      local v356 = { "Z", "X", "C" }

      pcall(function()
        virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, "RightControl", false, game)

        workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position, workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame.Position
          + Vector3.new(0, -1, 0))
      end)

      for index4, value49 in ipairs({ "Melee", "Blox Fruit", "Sword", "Gun" }) do
        local v357 = nil

        for key30, value50 in pairs(players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
          if value50:IsA("Tool") and value50.ToolTip == value49 then
            v357 = value50
            break
          end
        end

        if not v357 then
          local tool3 = players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")

          if tool3 and tool3.ToolTip == value49 then
            v357 = tool3
          end
        end

        if v357 then
          players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v357)
          task.wait(0.1)

          for m = 1, 3 do
            virtualUser:CaptureController()
            virtualUser:ClickButton1(Vector2.new(851, 158))

            local v358 = v356[m]

            if v358 then
              virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, v358, false, game)
              task.wait(0.05)
              virtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, v358, false, game)
            end

            task.wait(0.1)
          end
        end
      end
    end

    spawn(function()
      runService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if _G.AutoTyrant and _G.StartFarm then
          local character12 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

          if character12 then
            for key31, value51 in pairs(character12:GetDescendants()) do
              if value51:IsA("BasePart") and value51.CanCollide then
                value51.CanCollide = false
              end

            if character12:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
              character12.Humanoid.AutoRotate = true
              character12.Humanoid:ChangeState(11)
            end
          end
        end
      end)
    end)

    function f18()
      local value52 = players.LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value

      if value52 >= 2575 then
        return {
          Name = "Skull Slayer",
          QuestArgs = { "StartQuest", "TikiQuest3", 2 },
          QuestPos = CFrame.new(
            -16665.0879, 105.27478, 1577.61743, -0.999621451, 3.5280582e-8, 0.0275127869,
            3.5990368e-8, 1, 2.53032191e-8, -0.0275127869, 2.62838356e-8, -0.999621451
          ),
          FarmPos = CFrame.new(-16709.49, 419.68, 1751.09),
        }
      elseif value52 > 2550 then
        return {
          Name = "Serpent Hunter",

    function f19()
      local islandModel = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("TikiOutpost")
        and workspace.Map.TikiOutpost:FindFirstChild("IslandModel")

      local count = 0

      if islandModel then
        local eye1 = islandModel:FindFirstChild("Eye1")
        local eye2 = islandModel:FindFirstChild("Eye2")
        local islandChunks = islandModel:FindFirstChild("IslandChunks")

        local eye3 = islandChunks and islandChunks:FindFirstChild("E")
          and islandChunks.E:FindFirstChild("Eye3")

        local eye4 = islandChunks and islandChunks:FindFirstChild("E")
          and islandChunks.E:FindFirstChild("Eye4")

        if eye1 and eye1.Transparency == 0 then
          count = count + 1
        end

        if eye2 and eye2.Transparency == 0 then
          count = count + 1
        end

        if eye3 and eye3.Transparency == 0 then
          count = count + 1
        end

        if eye4 and eye4.Transparency == 0 then
          count = count + 1
        end
      end

      return count
    end

                          end

                          v412 = v415

                        until v412
                      elseif _G.SelectedIsland == "Cake" then
                        _tp(CFrame.new(-1943.6765, 251.5095, -12337.8808))
                      else
                        _tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806, 453.5862, 5977.3486))
                      end
                    else
                      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem", v414)
                    end

                    break
                  end

                  if tonumber(value101.Mastery) >= 600 then
                  end

                  break
                end
              end
            end
          end)
        end
      end)

    function f23()
      if not pcall(function()
        local jsonDecode = httpService:JSONDecode((game:HttpGet(string.format(
          "https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/%d/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100",
          game.PlaceId
        ))))

        if jsonDecode and jsonDecode.data then
          for index8, value111 in ipairs(jsonDecode.data) do
            if value111.playing < value111.maxPlayers and value111.id ~= game.JobId then
              local v419 = game

              teleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(
                game.PlaceId, value111.id, v419:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
              )

              return true
            end
          end

          return
        end
      end) then
        local v420 = game
        teleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, v420:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer)
      end
    end

                      * v320.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance), {
                      character19, workspaceService.Characters, workspaceService.Enemies,
                    })

                    local vector = v423 and Vector3.new(v423.X, math.max(v423.Y, v421), v423.Z)
                    local state = tool5:GetAttribute("State")
                    local serverState = tool5:GetAttribute("ServerState")

                    if vector and (state == "ReeledIn" or serverState == "ReeledIn") then
                      fishingRequest:InvokeServer("StartCasting")
                      task.wait()
                      fishingRequest:InvokeServer("CastLineAtLocation", vector, 100, true)
                    elseif serverState == "Biting" then
                      fishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catching", true)
                      task.wait(0.1)
                      fishingRequest:InvokeServer("Catch", 1)

                      ui:Notify({
                        Title = "Auto Fish",
                        Message = "New item caught",
                        Duration = 3,
                      })
                    end
                  end

                end

                ui:Notify({
                  Title = "Auto Fish",
                  Message = "please equip: " .. tostring(_G.SelectedRod),
                  Duration = 3,
                })

                return
              end

              if tool5 then
                local v424 = v321(humanoidRootPart21.Position)

                local v425, v426 = workspaceService:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(Ray.new(character19.Head.Position, humanoidRootPart21.CFrame.LookVector
                  * v320.Rod.MaxLaunchDistance), {
                  character19, workspaceService.Characters, workspaceService.Enemies,

        vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "F", false, game)
        vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "F", false, game)

      task.wait(0.2)

      while wait(0.5) do
        pcall(function()
          if _G.Prehis_Find then
            local character22 = plr.Character

            if not character22 then
              return
            else
              local humanoidRootPart23 = character22:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

              if not humanoidRootPart23 then
                return
              else
                local findFirstChild5 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild(
                  "Prehistoric Island", true
                )

                if not findFirstChild5 then
                  local v497 = CheckBoat()

                  if not v497 then
                    local cframe21 = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                    TeleportToTarget(cframe21)

                    if (cframe21.Position - humanoidRootPart23.Position).Magnitude <= 10 then
                      replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(
                        "BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat or "Guardian"
                      )
                    end

                    return
                  end

                  if character22.Humanoid.Sit == false then
                    _tp(v497.VehicleSeat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0))
                    return
                  end

                  cframe4 = CFrame.new(-10000000, 31, 37016.25)

                  if CheckEnemiesBoat() or CheckTerrorShark() or CheckPirateGrandBrigade() then
                    _tp(CFrame.new(-10000000, 150, 37016.25))
                  else
                    _tp(cframe4)
                  end

                  return
                else
                  local findFirstChild6 = findFirstChild5:FindFirstChild("HeadTeleport", true)
                    or findFirstChild5:FindFirstChild("Teleport_Head", true)
                    or findFirstChild5:FindFirstChild("Head", true)

                  if findFirstChild6 then
                    local v498 = findFirstChild6.CFrame.Position
                        - findFirstChild6.CFrame.LookVector * 40
                      + Vector3.new(0, 20, 0)

                    if (v498 - humanoidRootPart23.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
                      _tp(CFrame.new(v498))
                    end
                  else
                    local position7 = findFirstChild5.CFrame.Position

                    _tp(CFrame.new(position7
                        - (position7 - humanoidRootPart23.Position).Unit * 250
                      + Vector3.new(0, 60, 0)))
                  end

                  return
                end
              end
            end
          else
            return
          end
        end)
      end
    end)

    spawn(function()

            if workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Prehistoric Island", true) then

            local prehistoricIsland = workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("PrehistoricIsland")

            if not prehistoricIsland then
              return
            else
              for key69, value224 in pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
                if (value224:IsA("BasePart") or value224:IsA("MeshPart"))
                  and value224.Name:lower():find("lava") then
                  value224:Destroy()
                end
              end

              local core2 = prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("Core")

              if core2 then
                local interiorLava = core2:FindFirstChild("InteriorLava")

                if interiorLava then
                  interiorLava:Destroy()
                end
              end

              local trialTeleport = prehistoricIsland:FindFirstChild("TrialTeleport")

              for key70, value225 in pairs(prehistoricIsland:GetDescendants()) do
                if value225.Name == "TouchInterest"
                  and not (trialTeleport and value225:IsDescendantOf(trialTeleport)) then
                  value225.Parent:Destroy()
                end
              end

              return
            end
          end)
        end
      end
    end)

            local lavaGolem = GetConnectionEnemies("Lava Golem")

              repeat
                wait(0.1)
                _tp(lavaGolem.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                f25()
                lavaGolem.Humanoid:ChangeState(15)
                local v500 = not _G.Prehis_KillGolem

                v499 = v500
                v499 = v500 or not lavaGolem.Parent or lavaGolem.Humanoid.Health <= 0
              until v499
            end
          end)
        end
      end
    end)

    function f27()
      local v509, v510 = pcall(function()
        return replicated.Modules.Net["RF/DragonHunter"]:InvokeServer({ Context = "Check" })
      end)

      if v509 and v510 and v510.Text then
        if string.find(v510.Text, "Defeat") then
          local v511 = nil

          if string.find(v510.Text, "Hydra Enforcer") then
            v511 = "Hydra Enforcer"
          end

          if string.find(v510.Text, "Venomous Assailant") then
            v511 = "Venomous Assailant"
          end

          return "kill", v511
        end

        if string.find(v510.Text, "Destroy") then
          return "destroy", nil
        end

        return nil, nil
      end

      return nil, nil
    end

    function f28()
      for key73, value241 in pairs(plr.PlayerGui.Notifications:GetChildren()) do
        if value241.Name == "NotificationTemplate"
          and string.find(value241.Text, "Head back to the Dojo") then
          return true
        end
      end

      return false
    end

              if v512 == "kill" and v513 then
                local v516 = GetConnectionEnemies(v513)

                if v516 then

                    v515 = v517

                    v515 = v517 or not v516 or not v516.Parent or v516.Humanoid.Health <= 0
                      or f28()
                  until v515
                else
                  _tp(v513 == "Hydra Enforcer"
                      and CFrame.new(4620.6157226562, 1002.2954711914, 399.08688354492)
                    or CFrame.new(4674.92676, 1134.82654, 996.308838))
                end
              elseif v512 == "destroy" then
                local findFirstChild8 = workspace.Map.Waterfall.IslandModel:FindFirstChild(
                  "Meshes/bambootree", true
                )

                if findFirstChild8 then
                  repeat
                    wait()
                    _tp(findFirstChild8.CFrame * CFrame.new(4, 0, 0))

                    if (findFirstChild8.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 200 then
                      MousePos = findFirstChild8.Position
                      f25()
                    end

                    local v518 = not _G.FarmBlazeEM

                    v514 = v518
                    v514 = v518 or not v512 or f28()
                  until v514
                end
              end
            else
              _tp(CFrame.new(5813, 1208, 884))
            end
          end)
        end
      end
    end)

    function f29()
      if not workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("FishmanTrial") then
        return nil
      else
        local trialOfWater = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild("Trial of Water")

        if trialOfWater then
          for key80, value261 in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
            if value261:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and value261:FindFirstChild("Health")
              and value261.Health.Value > 0 then
              if (value261.HumanoidRootPart.Position - trialOfWater.Position).Magnitude <= 1500 then
                return value261
              end
            end
          end

          return nil
        end

        return nil
      end
    end

    fruitsCheckTab:AddSection({ "Fruits Options" })

    function f12()
      local v545 = [[
Advance Fruit Stock
]]

      local v546, v547 = pcall(function()
        return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", true)
      end)

      if v546 and v547 then
        local v548 = false

        for key83, value271 in pairs(v547) do
          if value271.OnSale then
            v548 = true
            v545 = v545 .. value271.Name .. " - $" .. f31(value271.Price) .. "\n"
          end
        end

        if not v548 then
          v545 = v545 .. [[
- No fruit in stock.
]]
        end
      else
        v545 = v545 .. [[
- Error retrieving data.
]]
      end

      local v549 = v545 .. "\nNormal Fruit Stock\n"

      local v550, v551 = pcall(function()
        return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")
      end)

      if v550 and v551 then
        local v552 = false

        for key84, value272 in pairs(v551) do
          if value272.OnSale then
            v552 = true
            v549 = v549 .. value272.Name .. " - $" .. f31(value272.Price) .. "\n"
          end
        end

        if not v552 then
          v549 = v549 .. [[
- No fruit in stock.
]]
        end
      else
        v549 = v549 .. [[
- Error retrieving data.
]]
      end

      return v549
    end

    function f31(p65)
      local v553 = tostring(p65)

      while true do
        local v554
        v554, k = v553:gsub("^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2")

        v553 = v554

        if k == 0 then
          break
        end
      end

      return v553
    end

      end
    end)

    function f32(p66)
      local v555, v556 = pcall(function()
        return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits", true)
      end)

      if v555 and v556 then
        for key86, value281 in pairs(v556) do
          if value281.Name == p66 and value281.OnSale then
            return true, value281.Price
          end
        end
      end

      local v557, v558 = pcall(function()
        return replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits")
      end)

      if v557 and v558 then
        for key87, value282 in pairs(v558) do
          if value282.Name == p66 and value282.OnSale then
            return true, value282.Price
          end
        end

        return false, nil
      end

      return false, nil
    end

    raidDungoenTab:AddSection({ "Auto Farm Dungeon" })
    v323 = { "Island 1", "Island 2", "Island 3", "Island 4", "Island 5" }

    function f13()
      local character25 = plr.Character
      return character25 and character25:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    function f14()
      local v563 = f13()

      if not v563 then
        return nil
      else
        local v564 = f33()

        if not v564 then
          return nil
        else
          local findFirstChild9 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild(v564)

          if not findFirstChild9 then
            return nil
          else
            local v565 = nil
            local huge9 = math.huge

            for key90, value293 in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
              local humanoid12 = value293:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
              local humanoidRootPart24 = value293:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

              if humanoid12 and humanoidRootPart24 and humanoid12.Health > 0 then
                if (humanoidRootPart24.Position - findFirstChild9.Position).Magnitude < 450 then
                  local magnitude11 = (humanoidRootPart24.Position - v563.Position).Magnitude

                  if magnitude11 < huge9 then
                    huge9 = magnitude11
                    v565 = value293
                  end
                end
              end
            end

            return v565
          end
        end
      end
    end

    function f33()
      local v566 = f13()

      if not v566 then
        return nil
      else
        local locations3 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations
        local huge10 = math.huge
        local v567 = nil

        for index13, value294 in ipairs(v323) do
          local findFirstChild10 = locations3:FindFirstChild(value294)

          if findFirstChild10 then
            local magnitude12 = (v566.Position - findFirstChild10.Position).Magnitude

            if magnitude12 < huge10 then
              huge10 = magnitude12
              v567 = value294
            end
          end
        end

        return v567, huge10
      end
    end

    function f16(p67)
      local findFirstChild11 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild(p67)

      if not findFirstChild11 then
        return false
      end

      for key91, value295 in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        local humanoid13 = value295:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local humanoidRootPart25 = value295:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if humanoid13 and humanoidRootPart25 and humanoid13.Health > 0 then
          if (humanoidRootPart25.Position - findFirstChild11.Position).Magnitude < 450 then
            return true
          end
        end
      end

      return false
    end

    function f15()
      local v568, v569 = f33()

      if not v568 then
        return false
      else
        local v570 = table.find(v323, v568)

        if not v570 or v570 >= #v323 then
          return false
        else
          local v571 = v323[v570 + 1]
          local findFirstChild12 = workspace._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild(v571)

          if findFirstChild12 then
            _tp(findFirstChild12.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 45, 120))
            return true
          end

          return false
        end
      end
    end

        if not _G.AutoDungeonFarm then
          currentIslandAddParagraph:SetDesc("None")
          mobsRemainingAddParagraph:SetDesc("0")
        else
          pcall(function()
            local raidTimer3 = plr.PlayerGui.Main.TopHUDList.RaidTimer

            if not raidTimer3 or not raidTimer3.Visible then
              currentIslandAddParagraph:SetDesc("Not in raid")
              mobsRemainingAddParagraph:SetDesc("0")
              return
            else
              local v572 = f13()

              if not v572 then
                return
              else
                local v573, v574 = f33()

                if v573 then
                  currentIslandAddParagraph:SetDesc(v573 .. " (" .. math.floor(v574) .. " studs)")
                end

                local count2 = 0

                for key92 in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                  count2 = count2 + 1
                end

                mobsRemainingAddParagraph:SetDesc(tostring(count2))
                local v575 = f14()

                if v575 then
                  local humanoidRootPart26 = v575:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                  if humanoidRootPart26 then
                    if (v572.Position - humanoidRootPart26.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
                      _tp(humanoidRootPart26.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                    end

                    if _G.SelectWeapon then
                      EquipWeapon(_G.SelectWeapon)
                    end

                    if v8 and v8.Kill then
                      v8.Kill(v575, true)
                    end
                  end
                elseif v573 and not f16(v573) then
                  f15()
                  task.wait(1)
                end

                return
              end
            end
          end)
        end
      end
    end)

              return
            else
              local character26 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

              if character26 then
                for key104, value338 in pairs(character26:GetDescendants()) do
                  if value338:IsA("BasePart") and value338.CanCollide then
                    value338.CanCollide = false
                  end
                end
              end

              return
            end
          end)
        else
          if _G.MeleeNoclip then
            _G.MeleeNoclip:Disconnect()
            _G.MeleeNoclip = nil
          end

          local character27 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

          if character27 then
            for key105, value339 in pairs(character27:GetDescendants()) do
              if value339:IsA("BasePart") then
                value339.CanCollide = true
              end
            end
          end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
          while _G.AutoBuyMelee do
            task.wait()

            if not _G.AutoBuyMelee then
              break
            else
              local v585 = v313[v335]

              if not v585 or not v585.Pos then
                ui:Notify({
                  Title = "Meo Lazy Hub",
                  Message = "Lỗi: Không tìm thấy toạ độ cho Melee này ở Sea hiện tại!",
                  Duration = 3,
                })

                _G.AutoBuyMelee = false
                break
              else
                local localPlayer19 = players.LocalPlayer

                if localPlayer19.Character
                  and localPlayer19.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                  local humanoidRootPart30 = localPlayer19.Character.HumanoidRootPart

                  if (humanoidRootPart30.Position - v585.Pos.Position).Magnitude > 15 then
                    _tp(v585.Pos)

                    replicatedStorage.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyItem", v585.Key)

                    ui:Notify({
                      Title = "Meo Lazy Hub",
                      Message = "Đã mua: " .. v335,
                      Duration = 2,
                    })

                    task.wait(1)
                  end
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end)

    tab:AddSection({ "Pvp, Aimbot, Movement" })

    function f34()
      local v591 = {}

      for index15, value353 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
        if value353.Name ~= players.LocalPlayer.Name then
          table.insert(v591, value353.Name)
        end
      end

      return v591
    end

    local selectPlayersAddDropdown = tab:AddDropdown({
      Name = "Select Players",
      Options = f34(),
      Default = nil,
      Multi = false,
      Callback = function(value354)
        _G.PlayersList = value354
        _G.SaveData.PlayersList = value354
        SaveSettings()
      end,
    })

              if findFirstChild16 and findFirstChild16.Character
                and findFirstChild16.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then

    tab:AddSection({ "Aimbot" })

    tab:AddToggle({
      Name = "Aimbot Cam Lock",
      Default = GetSetting("AimCam_Save", false),
      Callback = function(value357)
        _G.AimCam = value357
        _G.SaveData.AimCam_Save = value357

        SaveSettings()

        task.spawn(function()
          local currentCamera = workspace.CurrentCamera

          while _G.AimCam do
            pcall(function()
              local huge11 = math.huge
              local v592

              for index16, value358 in ipairs(players:GetPlayers()) do
                if value358 ~= players.LocalPlayer and value358.Character
                  and value358.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                  if value358.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                    local magnitude13 = (value358.Character.Head.Position
                      - players.LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude

                    if magnitude13 < huge11 then
                      v592 = value358
                      huge11 = magnitude13
                    end
                  end
                end
              end

              if v592 and v592.Character then
                currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                  currentCamera.CFrame.Position, v592.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
                )
              end
            end)

            task.wait()
          end
        end)
      end,
    })

    tab:AddToggle({
      Name = "Aimbot Skills",
      Default = GetSetting("SilentAim_Save", false),
      Callback = function(value359)
        _G.SilentAim = value359
        _G.SaveData.SilentAim_Save = value359
        SaveSettings()
      end,
    })

    tab:AddSection({ "Speed/Jump" })

    _G.WalkSpeedEnabled = _G.WalkSpeedEnabled or false
    _G.JumpEnabled = _G.JumpEnabled or false

    function f35()
      local character28 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

      if character28 then
        local humanoid15 = character28:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

        if humanoid15 then
          if _G.WalkSpeedEnabled then
            humanoid15.WalkSpeed = getgenv().WalkSpeedValue
          end

          if _G.JumpEnabled then
            humanoid15.JumpPower = getgenv().JumpValue
          end
        end
      end
    end

    function f36(p68)
      if p68 then
        p68:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
          if _G.WalkSpeedEnabled and p68.WalkSpeed ~= getgenv().WalkSpeedValue then
            p68.WalkSpeed = getgenv().WalkSpeedValue
          end
        end)
      end
    end

    local localPlayer20 = players.LocalPlayer

    localPlayer20.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character29)
      character29:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
      f35()
      f36(character29:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid"))
    end)

    if localPlayer20.Character then
      local humanoid16 = localPlayer20.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

      if humanoid16 then
        f35()
        f36(humanoid16)
      end
    end

    runService.Heartbeat:Connect(f35)

    tab:AddToggle({
      Name = "Set WalkSpeed",
      Default = GetSetting("SpeedToggle_Save", _G.WalkSpeedEnabled),
      Callback = function(value360)
        _G.WalkSpeedEnabled = value360
        _G.SaveData.SpeedToggle_Save = value360

        SaveSettings()
        f35()
      end,
    })

    tab:AddTextBox({

          getgenv().WalkSpeedValue = v593

          f35()
        end
      end,

      Callback = function(value363)

          getgenv().JumpValue = v594

    tab:AddSection({ "LocalPlayer Settings / Misc" })

    tab:AddToggle({
      Name = "Instance Mink V3 [ INF ]",
      Default = GetSetting("InfAblities_Save", false),

        _G.InfAblities = value364
        _G.SaveData.InfAblities_Save = value364

        SaveSettings()

        task.spawn(function()
          while _G.InfAblities do
            pcall(function()
              local character30 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

              if character30 and character30:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                if not character30.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Agility") then
                  local agility = replicatedStorage.FX.Agility:Clone()
                  agility.Name = "Agility"
                  agility.Parent = character30.HumanoidRootPart
                end
              end
            end)

            task.wait(0.2)
          end

          pcall(function()
            local character31 = players.LocalPlayer.Character

            if character31 and character31.HumanoidRootPart:FindFirstChild("Agility") then
              character31.HumanoidRootPart.Agility:Destroy()
            end
          end)
        end)
      end,
    })

                if value370.Name ~= players.LocalPlayer.Name then
                  commF3:InvokeServer("AcceptAlly", value370.Name)

    local function kq(fromCharacter, range)
      local result = {}
      local rootPart = fromCharacter and fromCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if not rootPart then
        return result
      end

      for _, folderName in ipairs({ "Enemies", "Characters" }) do
        local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)

        if folder then
          for _, model in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
            local humanoid = model:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            local modelRoot = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if humanoid and modelRoot and humanoid.Health > 0
              and model ~= localPlayer.Character
              and (modelRoot.Position - rootPart.Position).Magnitude <= range then
              table.insert(result, model)
            end
          end
        end
      end

      return result
    end

    function AttackNoCoolDown()
      local localPlayer21 = players.LocalPlayer
      local character32 = localPlayer21.Character
      local modules3

      if not character32 then
        return
      else
        local v595 = nil

        for index18, value371 in ipairs(character32:GetChildren()) do
          if value371:IsA("Tool") then
            v595 = value371
            break
          end
        end

        if not v595 then
          return
        else
          local v596 = kq(character32, 60)

          if #v596 == 0 then
            return
          end

          modules3 = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")

          if not modules3 then
            return
          else
            local reRegisterAttack2 = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net"):WaitForChild("RE/RegisterAttack")
            local net5 = replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Net")
            local v597 = not reRegisterAttack2
            local reRegisterHit2 = net5:WaitForChild("RE/RegisterHit")

            if v597 or not reRegisterHit2 then
              return
            else
              local v598 = {}
              local v599 = nil

              for index19, value372 in ipairs(v596) do
                if not value372:GetAttribute("IsBoat") then
                  local v600 = {
                    "RightLowerArm", "RightUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftUpperArm",
                    "RightHand", "LeftHand",
                  }

                  local findFirstChild18 = value372:FindFirstChild(v600[math.random(#v600)])
                    or value372.PrimaryPart

                  if findFirstChild18 then
                    table.insert(v598, { value372, findFirstChild18 })
                    v599 = findFirstChild18
                  end
                end
              end

              if not v599 then
                return
              else
                reRegisterAttack2:FireServer(0)
                local playerScripts = localPlayer21:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")

                if not playerScripts then
                  return
                else
                  local localScript = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")

                  while not localScript do
                    playerScripts.ChildAdded:Wait()
                    localScript = playerScripts:FindFirstChildOfClass("LocalScript")
                  end

                  local sendHitsToServer = nil

                  if getsenv then
                    local v601, v602 = pcall(getsenv, localScript)

                    if v601 and v602 then
                      sendHitsToServer = v602._G.SendHitsToServer
                    end
                  end

                  local v603, v604 = pcall(function()
                    return require(modules3.Flags).COMBAT_REMOTE_THREAD or false
                  end)

                  if v603 and v604 and sendHitsToServer then
                    sendHitsToServer(v599, v598)
                  elseif v603 and not v604 then
                    reRegisterHit2:FireServer(v599, v598)
                  end

                  return
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
    end

    CameraShakerR = require(replicatedStorage.Util.CameraShaker)
    CameraShakerR:Stop()

    function get_Monster()
      local myCharacter = localPlayer.Character
      local myRoot = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

      if not myRoot then
        return
      end

      for key109, value373 in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        local upperTorso = value373:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

        local head3 = upperTorso
        head3 = upperTorso or value373:FindFirstChild("Head")

        if value373:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart", true) and head3 then
          if (head3.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= 50 then
            return true, head3.Position
          end
        end
      end

      for key110, value374 in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
        if value374:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and value374:FindFirstChild("Health")
          and value374.Health.Value > 0 then
          return true, value374.HumanoidRootPart.Position
        end
      end

      for key111, value375 in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
        if value375:FindFirstChild("Health") and value375.Health.Value > 0
          and value375:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
          return true, value375.Engine.Position
        end
      end
    end

    function Actived()
      local v605, v606 = getconnections(players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool").Activated)

      for key112, value376 in next, v605, v606 do
        if typeof(value376.Function) == "function" then
          getupvalues(value376.Function)
        end
      end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
      runService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        pcall(function()
          if not _G.FruitM1 then
            return
          else
            AttackNoCoolDown()
            local tool6 = players.LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
            local v607, v608 = get_Monster()

            if tool6.ToolTip == "Blox Fruit" then
              if v607 then
                local leftClickRemote = tool6:FindFirstChild("LeftClickRemote")

                if leftClickRemote then
                  Actived()
                  leftClickRemote:FireServer(Vector3.new(0.01, -500, 0.01), 1, true)
                  leftClickRemote:FireServer(false)
                end
              end
            end

            return
          end
        end)
      end)
    end)

    return
  end
end
